clear

; ===========================================================================
; Age of Empires II: Definitive Edition mod (SoundRTS)
; Activate: mods = aoe2
;
; Primary references (player-facing Chinese names & civ bonuses):
;   https://wiki.biligame.com/aoe2de/首页
; https://liquipedia.net/ageofempires
;   https://aoe2.tw.com/   (site unreachable during build; used as intended source)
; Tech-tree availability cross-checked with SiegeEngineers aoe2techtree data:
;   https://github.com/SiegeEngineers/aoe2techtree
;
; Civs: britons 不列颠/ franks 法兰克/ chinese 中国 / mongols 蒙古 / byzantines 拜占庭; japanese 日本 / teutons 条顿 / vikings 维京 / vietnamese 越南 / portuguese 葡萄牙; aztecs 阿兹特克 / celts 凯尔特 / malians 马里
; AI: beginner intermediate advanced expert nightmare
; Resources (SoundRTS order): resource1=gold 黄金, resource2=wood 木材,
;   resource3=food 食物, resource4=stone 石料
; Hotkeys: z=gold(1) x=wood(2) Shift+Z=food(3) Shift+X=stone(4)
; Main base building: town_center (城镇中心), not townhall
; Costs/times: exact AoE2 DE values from SiegeEngineers aoe2techtree (gold wood food stone).
; Unit line upgrades use DE upgrade-research cost (not re-train cost).
; Combat stats: exact AoE2 DE HP/Attack/Armor/Range/Reload/Speed/LOS (SiegeEngineers aoe2techtree).
;
; ``clear`` wipes base ``res/rules.txt`` -> redeclare terrains and building_land below
; (maps still use nb_meadow_by_square / forest / town, etc.).
; ===========================================================================

; ---------- terrains (required after clear; same set as res/rules.txt) ----------

; AoE2 DE formations (line / box / staggered / flank). Spacing in meters.

ranks melee ranged siege

keep_pace 1

def formation_flank

ranks melee ranged siege
keep_pace 1

class armor

def cavalry
class armor

def archer_unit
class armor

def siege_unit
class armor

def unique_unit
class armor

; Category root for mdg_vs / rdg_vs / effect_bonus_targets building
; (battering ram, arson, masonry, town_watch, ...). Same pattern as infantry.
def building
class building
menace 0
; DE: first roll at CI 15, guaranteed at 25 (~8% per interval)
conversion_min_intervals 15
conversion_max_intervals 25
conversion_chance 8

; Archer attack + range + LOS (AoE2 DE Fletching line)

effect_bonus_targets archer_unit -hand_cannoneer galley scouttower aoe_castle
effect bonus sight_range 1
effect_bonus_targets archer_unit -hand_cannoneer galley scouttower aoe_castle
effect bonus rdg 1
effect_bonus_targets town_center townhall
effect bonus sight_range 1
effect_bonus_targets town_center townhall

; 征召 Conscription -> barracks/archery/stable/castle work +33% (train time -33%)
; DE (aoe2db): "Military buildings (except Siege Workshops) work +33% faster."
; Scope = units trained at Barracks / Archery Range / Stable / Castle /
; Krepost / Donjon.  Excluded: Dock (ships), Siege Workshop, Monastery /
; Fortified Church.
;
; The positive list names the four unit roots those buildings produce
; (infantry, archer_unit, cavalry) plus every civ-exclusive unique unit
; (unique_unit).  The explicit names add the Castle-trained siege units
; (Trebuchet, Petard), which are plain ``siege_unit`` and carry no
; unique-unit marker.
;
; The exclusions remove the categories DE never speeds up:
;   -ship             Dock units (Caravel, Turtle Ship, Dragon Ship, ...)
;   -monk             Monastery / Fortified Church units (Missionary, Warrior Priest)
;   -armored_elephant the Hindustani siege elephant (DE: Siege Workshop, not Castle)
;   -houfnice         Bohemian Bombard Cannon upgrade (DE: Siege Workshop)
;   -flaming_camel    Tatar siege unit (DE: Siege Workshop)
def conscription
class upgrade
cost 150 0 150 0
time_cost 60
requirements aoe_castle imperial_age
effect bonus time_cost -33%
effect_bonus_targets infantry archer_unit cavalry unique_unit trebuchet petard -ship -monk -armored_elephant -houfnice -flaming_camel

; 围城木栅 Hoardings — castle +1000 HP (DE castle tech; not 加厚墙 fortified_wall)
def hoardings
class upgrade
cost 0 400 400 0
time_cost 75
requirements aoe_castle imperial_age
effect bonus hp 1000
effect_bonus_targets aoe_castle
; DE: Hoardings also gives +1000 HP to the Sicilian Donjon.
effect bonus hp 1000
effect_bonus_targets donjon
; DE: Hoardings also gives +1000 HP to the Bulgarian Krepost.
effect bonus hp 1000
effect_bonus_targets krepost

; ===========================================================================
; Mounted Crossbowman line (AoE2 DE "The Viking Sagas" regional unit)
; ===========================================================================
; DE: a mounted archer that *replaces* the Cavalry Archer for twelve
; European civs (Bohemians, Britons, Burgundians, Celts, Franks, Italians,
; Poles, Portuguese, Sicilians, Spanish, Teutons, Vikings).  It is trained at
; the Archery Range in Castle Age and upgrades to the Heavy Mounted
; Crossbowman in Imperial Age.
;
; DE stats (unit 2700): 65 gold / 40 wood, 40s, HP 45, pierce 7 (+1 vs
; Spearmen), range 4, reload 2.5s (rdg_cd), attack delay 0.91s (rdg_ready),
; 80% accuracy, melee armour 2, pierce armour 0, speed 1.25, LoS 6.
; Heavy (unit 2701): 650 gold / 1000 food, 50s, HP 55, pierce 8, same
; range / reload, attack delay 0.996667s -> 1.0 (rdg_ready).
;
; The civ-specific stat variants (Frank 39 gold / 54 HP, Portuguese 52 gold
; / 65 HP / 2.25s reload, Teuton & Viking 1.25 speed, …) are applied by the
; owning civ's own definition further down in this file.
def mounted_crossbowman
class soldier
is_a archer_unit
is_a cavalry
cost 65 40 0 0
population_cost 2
time_cost 40
rdg_projectile 1
rdg_projectile_speed 7
rdg_targets ground
requirements castle_age
can_use_tech fletching bodkin_arrow bracer padded_archer_armor leather_archer_armor ring_archer_armor thumb_ring ballistics chemistry bloodlines husbandry cranequins conscription

hp_max 45
speed 1.25
sight_range 6
mdg 0
mdf 2
rdf 0
rdg 7

; ===========================================================================
; UNIQUE TECHNOLOGIES -> second wave (8 new civs x 2 = 16 techs)
; Costs/times DE-aligned where applicable. Where a target is a category root
; (e.g. ``infantry``), the bonus is applied via bonus stack to all subtypes.
; ===========================================================================

; 哥特 Perfusion — DE: "Barracks work +100% faster", researched at the Castle in
; the Imperial Age for 400 wood + 600 gold (40s).
; "Work +100% faster" means the Barracks produces its units twice as fast, i.e.
; the train time is halved (time_cost -50%).  The filter covers every unit the
; Barracks can produce: the militia line, the spearman line and - once Anarchy
; has unlocked it - the Huskarl line.
; DE Perfusion grants NO resource discount; the earlier "infantry -20% food"
; row was not in the original game and has been removed.
def perfusion
class upgrade
cost 600 400 0 0
time_cost 40
requirements aoe_castle imperial_age
effect bonus time_cost -50%
effect_bonus_targets militia spearman huskarl

; 哥特 Anarchy — DE: "Huskarls can be trained at Barracks."
; Once researched, the Barracks (goth_barracks) gains the Huskarl in its
; trainable list.  The effect is scoped to the Goth Barracks via
; ``effect_bonus_targets``; the barracks themselves list ``anarchy`` in
; ``can_use_tech`` for the effect to reach them.
;
; NOTE: only ``goth_barracks`` is listed.  Adding the generic ``barracks``
; would be redundant *and* wrong: every civ's Barracks inherits from it
; (``is_a barracks``), so the target would match all 41 civ Barracks —
; the very units the effect must NOT reach.  The target matcher resolves
; the ``is_a`` chain, so naming the derived type is enough.
def anarchy
class upgrade
cost 250 0 450 0
time_cost 40
requirements aoe_castle castle_age
effect bonus can_train huskarl 1
effect_bonus_targets goth_barracks

; 波斯 Kamandaran — Archery Range units cost gold converted from wood
; (DE: 1 wood -> 1 gold for the entire upgrade cost). Modelled as: archery range
; units cost 0 gold but pay 1 gold per 1 wood. We apply -100% gold cost on
; archer_unit and a per-cost adjustment for wood (handled by the global
; ``gather_byproduct``); simpler: gold→抴ood for archery units, modelled as
; ``cost 0 0 0 0`` on archer_unit with a side bonus for wood compensation.
def kamandaran
class upgrade
cost 300 0 400 0
time_cost 40
requirements aoe_castle castle_age
effect bonus cost -100% 0 0 0
effect_bonus_targets archer_unit -cavalry
; Lumberjacks generate 30 gold per wood as compensation
effect bonus gather_byproduct wood 0.30
effect_bonus_targets peasant

; 波斯 Silk Road — Trade carts generate +15% gold; markets cost -50% wood
; D-Phase 2: ``trade_reward_bonus_pct`` is now a real effect bonus stat
; (engine stores it on the player and applies it in ``_credit_reward``).
; Market wood discount via ``effect bonus cost`` (engine supports).
def silk_road
class upgrade
cost 250 0 250 0
time_cost 60
requirements aoe_castle castle_age
effect bonus trade_reward_bonus_pct 15
effect bonus cost 0 -50% 0 0
effect_bonus_targets market

; 斯拉夫德里齐纳 Druzhina — Farmers generate gold when working (DE: 1 gold per 10
; food). Modelled as gather byproduct.
def druzhina
class upgrade
cost 500 0 900 0
time_cost 40
requirements aoe_castle imperial_age
effect bonus gather_byproduct farm resource1 0.10
effect_bonus_targets peasant

; 斯拉夫东正教 Orthodoxy — Barracks units +5/+0/+0/+0/+3 attack/Range— DE: infantry
; +3/+0/+0/+0/+3 attack bonus; we model as +3 attack and +1 range for spearman line.

; 哥特 Huskarl Loot — Huskarl line +5 gold per kill against infantry.
; D-Phase 2: stacked on top of base target.resource_rewards; also
; multiplied by faction ``kill_resource_bonus_pct`` (goths +30%).
; Targets ``infantry`` covers militia / man_at_arms / champion / spearman /
; pikeman / halberdier / huskarl itself via expanded_is_a lookup.

; 钦察 Cuman Mercenaries -> Player may build a second Town Center in Castle Age
; after researching this tech. D-Phase 2: engine supports ``allow_extra_town_center``
; as a player flag, so the upgrade now lifts ``player.town_center_max`` by 1.
def cuman_mercenaries
class upgrade
cost 400 0 650 0
time_cost 40
requirements aoe_castle imperial_age
allow_extra_town_center 1

; Mill farm upgrades (重犁 / 连枷 / 轮作)
; DE: "New Farms provide +75 food.  Existing Farms provide a smaller food
; amount."  The bonus is written as a normal ``effect bonus`` row on the
; farm's food capacity (``resource_volume_max`` is the engine's stat for a
; gather building's buffer cap) instead of the old bespoke
; ``farm_food_bonus`` field.  ``effect_bonus_targets farm`` restricts the
; row to farms, so the tech does not affect other gather buildings.
; The retroactive "existing farms get a smaller amount" part (DE scales it
; with the square of the remaining-food fraction) is handled by
; ``soundrts.world_build_rules._apply_farm_food_tech_bonuses``.
;
; The ``effect_bonus_targets`` list of a filtered row *is* its authorisation:
; the farm therefore does not have to repeat 马轭/重犁/轮作 in its
; ``can_use_tech`` — the target list alone already says "farms enjoy this".
; That is why the target list must never name a base class next to one of
; its descendants: e.g. the Goths' ``anarchy`` targets ``goth_barracks``,
; whose base ``barracks`` is the ancestor of *every* civ's Barracks, so
; naming the base there would reach all 41 of them (see
; ``soundrts/worldupgrade/effect_bonus_parse.py``).
def horse_collar
class upgrade
cost 0 75 75 0
time_cost 20
requirements mill feudal_age
effect bonus resource_volume_max 75
effect_bonus_targets farm

def heavy_plow
class upgrade
cost 0 125 125 0
time_cost 40
requirements mill castle_age horse_collar
effect bonus resource_volume_max 125
effect_bonus_targets farm
; DE: farmers carry +1 food
effect bonus carry_capacity 1
effect_bonus_targets peasant

def crop_rotation
class upgrade
cost 0 250 250 0
time_cost 70
requirements mill imperial_age heavy_plow
effect bonus resource_volume_max 175
effect_bonus_targets farm

; 城楼 Donjon (Sicilian unique fortification, DE Lords of the West)
; DE: 50 wood / 175 stone, buildable from the Dark Age, 2x2 footprint.
; It *replaces* the Watch Tower line for the Sicilians and is the building that
; unlocks the Archery Range and the Stable.
; HP by age: 625 (Dark) / 1250 (Feudal) / 1500 (Castle) / 2250 (Imperial).
; Attack by age: none (Dark) / 1 arrow (Feudal) / 2 arrows (Castle) / 3 arrows
; (Imperial).  Garrison: 10 units, heals them.
; Trains the Serjeant (Unique Unit) and the Spearman line.
def donjon
class building
is_a building
; DE: the Donjon is a 2x2 building, so it may not be placed on the 1x1 meadow
; slot the towers use.

; ===========================================================================
; DE UNIQUE BUILDINGS (缺失的 9 个独有建筑)
; ===========================================================================
; DE 的 6 种独有 / 区域建筑，覆盖 9 个文明槽位：
;   Krepost         保加利亚 (Castle Age)
;   Caravanserai    波斯 + 印度 (Imperial Age)
;   Folwark         波兰 (Dark Age，替换 Mill)
;   Mule Cart       亚美尼亚 + 格鲁吉亚 (Dark Age，替换 Lumber/Mining Camp)
;   Fortified Church 亚美尼亚 + 格鲁吉亚 (Castle Age，替换 Monastery)
;   Settlement      印加 (Dark Age，替换 Mill/Lumber/Mining Camp)
;
; 所有数值取自 DE 官方数据（_aoe2techtree_data.json 的 Building 表 +
; _de_trees/*.json 的科技树节点）。

; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
; 城堡 Krepost — 保加利亚独有要塞 (DE id 1251)
; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
; DE: HP 2600 / Melee 8 / Pierce 11 / Atk 10 / Range 7 / LOS 10 /
;     Garrison 20 / 350 stone / 150s / Castle Age / 提供 20 人口
; DE 科技树 (bulgarians.json): Konnik + Elite Konnik + Petard
; DE 官方说明: "Unique fortification of the Bulgarians used to train your
; Unique Unit. Units can garrison inside for protection. Ranged Soldiers
; and Villagers shoot additional projectiles. Provides 20 population.
; Cannot be converted by enemy Monks."

sight_range 10

rdg 10
rdg_cd 2
rdg_range 7
rdg_minimal_range 1
rdg_projectile 1
rdg_projectile_speed 7
; DE: garrison 20 (foot units + villagers + monks + cavalry).
transport_capacity 20
transport_volume 99
transport_passenger_types infantry archer_unit peasant monk cavalry
passenger_attack_types archer_unit peasant
; DE: the Krepost trains the Bulgarian unique unit and the Petard.

; AoE2 Monk (replaces fantasy priest kit when trained from monastery)
def monk
class soldier
requirements castle_age
cost 100 0 0 0
population_cost 1
time_cost 51
mana_max 100
; DE: ~1.6 faith/s -> empty to full ~ 62.5s; Illumination x1.875 ~ 33.3s
mana_regen 1.6
mana_regen_cd 1
;space 0.25
; DE: ~150 HP/min ~ 2.5 HP/s ~ 1 HP / 0.4s (AoEZone)
heal_level 1
heal_cd 0.4
; Relics / campaign items (pick up, carry to monastery)
inventory_capacity 1
receive_items 1
; Rules-driven conversion gates (no engine tech-name checks)
conversion_tech_gated 1
conversion_cleric 1
can_use_tech bimaristan first_crusade fereters

hp_max 30
speed 0.7
sight_range 11
mdg 0
mdf 0
rdf 0
rdg 0
; rdg_cd 0 / rdg_range 0 -- monk has rdg 0 (no ranged attack); the
; "ranged" operations monks perform (a_conversion skill / heal_*) are
; driven by their own cooldowns (a_conversion time_cost 6, heal_cd 0.4)
; and ranges (effect_range 9 / heal_range 8) -- NOT by the rdg_cd /
; rdg_range pair. Setting rdg_cd 0 / rdg_range 0 here keeps the
; unit-attribute screen from showing misleading "ranged cooldown 1.6 /
; ranged range 9" lines on a unit that has no ranged weapon, and stops
; the AI from treating monks as ranged units.
rdg_cd 0
rdg_range 0
can_use_skill a_conversion
can_use_tech sanctity fervor illumination block_printing redemption atonement

def relic
class item
transport_volume 1
inventory_production_rates 0.5 0 0 0
inventory_victory 1

; 传教士 Missionary — Spanish unique unit (AoE2 DE / The Conquerors).
; A *mounted* monk trained at the Monastery in Castle Age for 100 gold, 51s.
; There is NO Elite Missionary in AoE2 DE — unlike every other UU line, the
; Missionary does not upgrade (AoE2 wiki: "Unlike most unique units,
; Missionaries do not have an Elite version").
; DE stats: HP 30, Attack 0, Armor 0/0, Heal 1 HP / 0.8s (half the monk rate),
; Speed 1.1, LoS 9, Train 51s, Cost 100 gold.  It cannot pick up Relics.
def missionary
class soldier
is_a monk
is_a unique_unit
requirements monastery castle_age
cost 100 0 0 0

time_cost 51
no_auto_upgrade 1
; The Missionary cannot collect Relics -> no inventory capacity.

receive_items 0
mana_max 100
mana_regen 1.6
mana_regen_cd 1
;space 0.25
; DE: half of monk heal rate ~ 1 HP / 0.8s
heal_level 1
heal_cd 0.8
hp_max 30
speed 1.1
sight_range 9
mdg 0
mdf 0
rdf 0
rdg 0
; rdg_cd 0 / rdg_range 0 -- missionary (Spanish mounted monk) inherits
; is_a monk, so it already picks up the rdg_cd 0 / rdg_range 0 from
; monk. The previous rdg_cd 1.6 / rdg_range 7 was overriding that
; with no purpose: missionary has rdg 0 (no ranged attack) and its
; "ranged" operations (a_conversion / heal_*) use their own cooldowns
; and ranges, exactly like monk. See the comment on def monk.
rdg_cd 0
rdg_range 0
can_use_skill a_conversion
can_use_tech bimaristan first_crusade fereters sanctity fervor illumination block_printing redemption atonement
can_use_tech herbal_medicine heresy theocracy inquisition
; AoE2 DE: the Missionary converts like a monk and benefits from the
; Spanish Inquisition (Monks and Missionaries convert faster).
can_upgrade_to

; ---------- Ages (AoE2 DE) ----------
; Costs/times match DE. No universal combat phase bonuses (res demo cleared).
; Building HP/armor age tables (DE) applied here as additive phase bonus groups;
; bases are dark/unlock-age on each building. Civ-specific rewards stay on race on_phase.
; Unit lines use researched ``line_upgrade`` (not age ``units_auto_upgrade``).
def dark_age
class phase
cost 0 0 0 0
time_cost 0
phase bonus
phase_bonus_targets
units_auto_upgrade 0
can_upgrade_to feudal_age

def feudal_age
class phase
cost 0 0 500 0
time_cost 130
requirements dark_age

phase bonus clear
phase bonus hp 200 mdf 1 rdf 1

phase bonus hp 300 mdf 1 rdf 1

phase bonus mdf 1 rdf 1

phase bonus mdf 1 rdf 1
phase_bonus_targets shipyard
phase bonus sight_range 2
phase_bonus_targets outpost
can_upgrade_to castle_age

; ===========================================================================
; CIVILIZATIONS -> bonuses from current AoE2 DE wiki (Fandom civ bonus list)
; Cross-check: biligame names, SiegeEngineers aoe2techtree, liquipedia.
; Base template: ``is_a Civilization`` inherits starting_resources / starting_units.
; A civ may override either field; omit to keep the Civilization defaults.
; ``abstract 1`` keeps Civilization out of the faction picker.
; ===========================================================================

def Civilization
class race
abstract 1
starting_resources 100 200 200 200
; DE Dark Age: Town Center, 3 villagers, 1 scout. No house (TC pop 5).
; Chinese overrides to 6 villagers + scout; Aztecs replace scout with eagle.
starting_units town_center 3 peasant scout_cavalry

; AI ``get footman/archer/knight`` must map to dark/feudal trainables
; (barracks / archery_range / stables). Castle unique units stay castle-only.

on_phase imperial_age rdg_range 1 aoe_archer crossbowman arbalester longbowman elite_longbowman
; DE: shepherds +25% -> livestock gather time x 1/1.25 ~ -20%
on_phase dark_age gather_time_food_livestock -20%
; DE: Town Centers -50% wood from Castle Age
on_phase castle_age cost 0 -50% 0 0 town_center
; DE Team bonus: Archery Ranges work +10% faster
team_on_phase dark_age time_cost -9% aoe_archer crossbowman arbalester longbowman elite_longbowman skirmisher elite_skirmisher imperial_skirmisher cavalry_archer heavy_cavalry_archer mangudai elite_mangudai

; ===========================================================================
; 9 MISSING <civ>_villager DEFS (Option A) + 1 missing race def (Option B
; Saracens) + 9 race-segment missing references fixed (Option C).
; 26 civs already had their race defs in lines 6038-8246, but 9 of them
; still used the default `peasant peasant` and therefore lacked a per-civ
; villager (so the civ could not actually build civ-specific structures
; from its own villagers). These new defs close that gap.
;
; Saracens (27th AoE2 DE civ) was the only civ with no race def at all.
; ===========================================================================


; ---------- Britons (Britannia) ----------
; DE: no Plate Barding, no Two-Handed Sword, no Champion, no Hand Cannoneer,
; no Heavy Cavalry Archer, no Thumb Ring, no Bloodlines. Archers +1/+2 range
; Castle/Imperial (already in race on_phase).
; Villager: standard eco techs via base `peasant` (aoe2-b style).


; ---------- Byzantines (Byzantine Empire) ----------
; DE: no Blast Furnace, no Bloodlines, no Halberdier (per current wiki - but
; the existing byzantine_barracks still lists it; leaving it there so the
; race stays consistent with the existing building def).  No plate barding
; was already accounted for.
; Villager: standard eco techs via base `peasant` (aoe2-b style).


; ---------- Japanese (日本) ----------
; DE: no Plate Barding, no Bombard Tower, no Heated Shot, no Crop Rotation,
; no Stone Shaft Mining, no Architecture, no Sappers. Villager is fine to
; keep the standard economic techs; the difference is in the military
; building can_train/can_research lists (already enforced in
; japanese_barracks/archery/stable/university/castle).
; Villager: standard eco techs via base `peasant` (aoe2-b style).


; ---------- Teutons (条顿) ----------
; DE: no Bracer, no Thumb Ring, no Husbandry, no Light Cavalry / Hussar,
; no Architecture, no Gold Shaft Mining, no Shipwright, no Dry Dock.
; Villager keeps the standard eco line minus gold_shaft_mining.
; Villager: standard eco techs via base `peasant` (aoe2-b style).


; ---------- Vikings (维京) ----------
; DE: no Bloodlines, no Thumb Ring, no Husbandry, no Plate Barding,
; no Halberdier, no Bombard Tower, no Shipwright, no Stone Shaft Mining,
; no Guilds. Standard eco line minus stone_shaft_mining.
; Villager: standard eco techs via base `peasant` (aoe2-b style).


; ---------- Vietnamese (越南) ----------
; DE: no Blast Furnace, no Gambesons, no Parthian Tactics, no Architecture,
; no Heavy Cavalry Archer, no Siege Ram, no Gold Shaft Mining, no Shipwright.
; Imperial Skirmisher shared with generic skirmisher line. Eco line
; follows the existing stable standards.
; Villager: standard eco techs via base `peasant` (aoe2-b style).


; ---------- Celts (凯尔特— ----------
; DE: no Two-Handed Sword, no Champion, no Arbalester, no Thumb Ring,
; no Cavalry Archer, no Hand Cannoneer, no Bloodlines, no Hussar,
; no Paladin, no Plate Barding, no Bracer, no Architecture, no Bombard
; Tower, no Crop Rotation, no Cannon Galleon. Ring Archer Armor YES.
; Villager has full standard eco techs.
; Villager: standard eco techs via base `peasant` (aoe2-b style).


; ---------- Malians (马里) ----------
; DE: no Gambesons, no Halberdier, no Blast Furnace, no Bracer, no Plate
; Barding, no Arbalester, no Heavy Cavalry Archer, no Hand Cannoneer,
; no Paladin, no Hussar, no Parthian Tactics, no Architecture, no Siege
; Engineers, no Bombard Tower, no Siege Ram, no Galleon, no Elite Cannon
; Galleon, no Illumination. Villager keeps standard eco techs.
; Villager: standard eco techs via base `peasant` (aoe2-b style).


; ===========================================================================
; OPTION B -> Saracens (27th civ) complete race def + villager.
; No Saracen units/buildings were ever defined in this rules.txt, so the
; whole segment is added fresh. Existing mod shared types are referenced
; (aoe_archer, aoe_knight, mangonel, aoe_castle, aoe_dock, market,
; aoe_battering_ram, etc.) plus the standard barracks / archery / stables
; / blacksmith / university / workshop / shipyard / monastery / town_center
; base classes.
;
; Saracens DE bonuses (from a combined biligame / liquipedia check):
;  - Market trade gold +25% (handled in market logic - already general)
;  - Transport ships double HP, +5 carry, fire +2 arrows (handled in
;    `transport_ship` / `aoe_dock` race-neutral code)
;  - Galleys attack bonus vs. buildings (+1 attack, already in unit defs)
;  - Foot archers +1 attack vs. buildings team bonus
;  - Castle unique unit: Mameluke (Castle) / Elite Mameluke (Imperial)
;  - Unique techs: Zealotry (Castle: +30 HP camel rider / mameluke / saracen
;    knight line) / Madrasah (Imperial: monks return 33% gold on death)
;
; The Mameluke / Elite Mameluke unit types are not yet defined in this mod.
; To keep the race playable we register the saracen race now and leave a
; `mameluke` placeholder referencing aoe_knight. A future commit should
; add the real Mameluke unit def.
; ===========================================================================
; Villager: standard eco techs via base `peasant` (aoe2-b style).

; Mameluke (Saracen Castle unique unit, DE wiki)
; - Cavalry / camel chassis (is_a camel_rider -> cavalry, 2 pop, speed 1.45)
; - Throwing swords: physical/melee-type damage dealt via a projectile
;   (5把掷斧/长矛投射斧 — same as Gbeto). Damage type is melee (mdg channel),
;   NOT ranged (rdf), so the engine:
;     * applies melee armor (mdf) on the target, not ranged (rdf)
;     * applies melee attack upgrades (forging/iron_casting/blast_furnace)
;     * applies melee-defence techs vs the attacker
;   The projectile just extends the delivery range (3/4 tiles) - the
;   damage type stays melee.
; - Castle Age: HP 80, melee dmg 7, reload 2.0s, range 3
; - Imperial (Elite): HP 130, melee dmg 11, reload 1.65s, range 4
; - Camel bonus vs cavalry preserved via is_a camel_rider (mdg_vs cavalry 9/18)
; - Population cost 2 (camel tier)
def mameluke
class soldier
is_a camel_rider
is_a unique_unit
cost 55 0 60 0
population_cost 2
time_cost 23
;space 0.45
mdg_projectile 1
mdg_projectile_speed 5
mdg_targets ground
requirements castle_age
can_use_tech stirrups silk_armor maghrebi_camels manipur_cavalry first_crusade aznauri_cavalry

hp_max 80
speed 1.45
sight_range 5
mdg 7
mdg_cd 2.0
mdg_ready 1
mdg_range 3
mdf 0
rdf 0
; Camel bonus vs cavalry: keep parent's mdg_vs cavalry 9 (camel_rider)
can_use_tech forging iron_casting blast_furnace scale_barding_armor chain_barding_armor plate_barding_armor bloodlines husbandry conscription
can_upgrade_to elite_mameluke

; DE Sicilians (Lords of the West) — the official DE civilization bonus list is:
;   * Start with +100 stone
;   * Farm upgrades provide +125% additional food
;   * Soldiers receive -40% bonus damage  (mdf +25% / rdf +25%)
;   * Can build Donjon in Dark Age, replaces Watch Tower line
;   * Fortifications built 50% faster; Town Centers built 100% faster
; Team bonus: Transport Ships +5 line of sight and cost -50%
;
; NOTE: the Sicilians have **no** building-HP bonus and **no** Town Center HP
; bonus in DE (those belong to the Byzantines' "buildings +10/20/30/40% HP").
; The Donjon's own age scaling is listed further below.
; DE: military units take -40% bonus damage (mdf +25% / rdf +25%)
on_phase dark_age mdf 25% infantry cavalry archer_unit siege_unit
on_phase dark_age rdf 25% infantry cavalry archer_unit siege_unit
; DE: farmers +30% faster (gather_time -23%)
on_phase dark_age gather_time -23% food
; DE: start with +100 stone (200 -> 300 stone, 4th resource slot).
starting_resources 100 200 200 300
; DE 城楼 Donjon: replaces the Watch Tower line, buildable from the Dark Age.
; DE HP by age: 625 (Dark) / 1250 (Feudal) / 1500 (Castle) / 2250 (Imperial).
; The Donjon also gains +1 melee / +1 pierce armor per age (DE).
;
; NOTE: the age rows use *flat* HP deltas, not percentages.  A percentage
; row (``hp 100%``) is relative to the unit's *current* HP, so it compounds
; across ages (625 -> 1250 -> 2500 ...) and the Dark Age row would give the
; Dark Age Donjon 1250 HP.  DE's numbers are exact totals, so the flat
; deltas below reproduce them exactly:
;   Dark 625 (base) | Feudal +625 -> 1250 | Castle +250 -> 1500
;   | Imperial +750 -> 2250
on_phase feudal_age hp 625 donjon
on_phase castle_age hp 250 donjon
on_phase imperial_age hp 750 donjon
on_phase feudal_age mdf 1 rdf 1 donjon
on_phase castle_age mdf 1 rdf 1 donjon
on_phase imperial_age mdf 1 rdf 1 donjon
; DE 城楼 Donjon attack by age: none (Dark) / 1 arrow (Feudal) / 2 arrows
; (Castle) / 3 arrows (Imperial).  Each age adds one extra projectile.
; A bare ``rdg_seq_times`` is a no-op in this engine: the sequence only runs
; when the secondary mode is on (``rdg_seq_secondary``) and the extra shots
; carry the live main damage (``rdg_seq_secondary_live``).
; The base ``rdg``/``rdg_range`` live on the ``def donjon`` block (5 pierce,
; range 8); only the Dark Age Donjon is unarmed, which is modelled by the
; lack of any attack row in the dark_age phase.
on_phase feudal_age rdg 5 donjon
on_phase feudal_age rdg_seq_times 1 donjon
on_phase feudal_age rdg_seq_secondary 1 donjon
on_phase feudal_age rdg_seq_secondary_live 1 donjon
on_phase castle_age rdg_seq_times 2 donjon
on_phase castle_age rdg_seq_secondary 1 donjon
on_phase castle_age rdg_seq_secondary_live 1 donjon
on_phase imperial_age rdg_seq_times 3 donjon
on_phase imperial_age rdg_seq_secondary 1 donjon
on_phase imperial_age rdg_seq_secondary_live 1 donjon
; DE: Fortifications built 50% faster; Town Centers built 100% faster.
; DE expresses these as a *time reduction*: the Donjon takes 125 s base and
; 83 s with the bonus (125 / 1.5), and a Town Center takes 100 s base and
; 50 s with the bonus (100 / 2).  This repo models "N% faster" as a time
; *discount* (see the Hindustani Grand Trunk Road "+25% faster" -> -20%
; row), so +50% faster -> -33% and +100% faster -> -50%.
; NOTE: ``time_cost`` in an ``on_phase`` row is a player-level bonus and is
; NOT filtered by the row's unit targets (the engine warns about this), so
; the per-building-type build speed is expressed through the phase pool's
; targeted ``merge_pool_time_for_type`` path instead — see the
; ``phase_time_cost`` handling in world_civ_bonuses.
on_phase dark_age time_cost -33% donjon wall fortified_wall palisade_wall palisade_gate outpost aoe_castle cannontower
on_phase dark_age time_cost -50% town_center
; DE Team bonus: Transport Ships +5 line of sight and cost -50%
; (transport_ship costs 0 125 0 0 -> the discount belongs in slot 1 = wood)
team_on_phase dark_age sight_range 5 transport_ship
team_on_phase dark_age cost 0 -50% 0 0 transport_ship
beginner sicilians_beginner
intermediate sicilians_intermediate
advanced sicilians_advanced
expert sicilians_expert
nightmare sicilians_nightmare

; DE: Galley-line and Dromons fire an additional projectile.
;
; Engine semantics (verified in soundrts/combat/damage_effects.py
; ::_schedule_ballistic_hit and soundrts/combat/attack_action.py
; ::_get_volley_attack_cooldown):
;   * A bare ``*_seq_times`` is a NO-OP.  The engine only runs a multi-shot
;     sequence when either an explicit damage split is configured
;     (``*_seq_damages``) or the *secondary* mode is on
;     (``*_seq_secondary``).  Without it the shot count is forced back to 1.
;   * The Galley-line fires with its ranged attack, so it needs the ``rdg``
;     sequence; the Dromon has no ranged attack at all (it is a
;     ``mdg_projectile`` melee unit), so it needs the ``mdg`` sequence.
;   * ``*_seq_secondary_live`` makes every extra shot reuse the unit's live
;     attack value, so the bonus doubles the damage like the DE extra
;     projectile instead of using a stale snapshot.
on_phase dark_age rdg_seq_times 2 galley war_galley galleon
on_phase dark_age rdg_seq_secondary 1 galley war_galley galleon
on_phase dark_age rdg_seq_secondary_live 1 galley war_galley galleon
on_phase dark_age mdg_seq_times 2 dromon
on_phase dark_age mdg_seq_secondary 1 dromon
on_phase dark_age mdg_seq_secondary_live 1 dromon
; DE bonus not expressible in this mod's rule language:
;   * "Mule Carts cost -25%" / "Mule Cart technology effects +40%" --
;     the mod has no Mule Cart building (it uses the generic Trade Cart).
;   * "Spearman- and Militia-line upgrades (except Man-at-Arms) available
;     one age earlier" -- needs a per-civ age override on the unit
;     classes; the mod has no such override primitive.
;   * "First Fortified Church receives a free Relic" -- the engine has
;     no "grant a relic to the first building" primitive.
; DE Team bonus: Infantry +2 line of sight
team_on_phase dark_age sight_range 2 militia man_at_arms long_swordsman two_handed_swordsman spearman pikeman halberdier huskarl elite_huskarl serjeant elite_serjeant condottiero obuch elite_obuch warrior_priest jaguar_warrior elite_jaguar_warrior
beginner armenians_beginner
intermediate armenians_intermediate
advanced armenians_advanced
expert armenians_expert
nightmare armenians_nightmare

; DE: Mounted Units regenerate 2/8/14 HP per minute in Feudal/Castle/Imperial Age.
;
; Engine semantics (verified in soundrts/worldunit/world_status_update.py
; ::regenerate and soundrts/worldupgrade/attribute_effects.py):
;   * hp_regen is PRECISION-scaled (x1000) and holds the HP healed per
;     tick; hp_regen_cd is the tick interval in ms.  Both are scaled by
;     1000 at rules-load time, so "60" means 60 000 ms = 60 s.
;   * on_phase rows are CUMULATIVE: each age's row is pushed onto the
;     player's _phase_bonus_pool and the whole pool is re-applied to
;     every unit on every age-up.  Every row must therefore state the
;     DELTA that the age adds, not the DE total.
;   * hp_regen_cd is additive too, so it is stated ONCE (Feudal row)
;     -- repeating it would multiply the regeneration interval.
;
; DE totals per minute: Feudal 2, Castle 8, Imperial 14
;   -> deltas           : Feudal +2, Castle +6, Imperial +6
;   -> per 60 s tick    : hp_regen 2 / 8 / 14, hp_regen_cd 60
on_phase feudal_age hp_regen 2 hp_regen_cd 60 cavalry
on_phase castle_age hp_regen 6 cavalry
on_phase imperial_age hp_regen 6 cavalry
; DE Team bonus: Building repairs cost -25%
team_repair_cost_pct -25
beginner georgians_beginner
intermediate georgians_intermediate
advanced georgians_advanced
expert georgians_expert
nightmare georgians_nightmare

; DE (Return of Rome) tech tree, verified against the DE data + the official
; patch notes (Update 83607 / "Romans: Lose access to Arson").
; Barracks: Militia -> Man-at-Arms -> Long Swordsman -> Legionary.  The Legionary
;   is an Imperial Age *barracks* unit that replaces Two-Handed Swordsman AND
;   Champion, so the Romans have neither.  They DO have Pikeman + Halberdier.
;   No Arson (lost in a balance update), no Gambesons, no Supplies.
; Archery: Archer -> Crossbowman (no Arbalester); Skirmisher + Elite Skirmisher;
;   Cavalry Archer + Heavy Cavalry Archer; no Hand Cannoneer, no Thumb Ring,
;   no Parthian Tactics.
; Stable: Scout -> Light Cavalry -> Knight -> Cavalier.  No Hussar, no Paladin.
; Blacksmith: no Bracer, no Plate Mail Armor (and no Plate Barding Armor).
; Siege: no Siege Onager, no Bombard Cannon.
; Monastery: no Theocracy, no Heresy, no Sanctity.
; Castle: Centurion (Castle) + Elite Centurion (Imperial), Petard, Trebuchet,
;   Conscription, Sappers, Spies.  No Hoardings.
; University: no Bombard Tower, no Heated Shot, no Dry Dock, no Siphons,
;   no Incendiaries, no Treadmill Crane.
; Economy: no Two-Man Saw, no Stone Shaft Mining.
; UT: Ballistas (castle), Comitatenses (castle).

is_a barracks
can_train militia spearman
can_research tracking squires man_at_arms long_swordsman pikeman halberdier legionary

can_research crossbowman elite_skirmisher heavy_cavalry_archer

; ---------- 罗马 Legionary (UU) ----------
; DE (post-Chronicles patch 141935): HP 75, Atk 12, Arm 2/2, speed 0.96,
; LoS 5, train 21s.  Imperial Age *barracks* line-upgrade that replaces
; Two-Handed Swordsman / Champion, so there is no Elite version.
; Researched at the Roman Barracks: 400 gold / 800 food, 100s.
; Trained at the sword-line price (20 gold / 50 food, 21s) like every tier.
; +4 vs infantry, +4 vs standard buildings.
; ``is_a long_swordsman`` puts the Legionary in the sword line so the engine
; resolves its train cost / train time from the line root (militia), exactly
; like the Champion does via ``is_a two_handed_swordsman``.
def legionary
class soldier
is_a long_swordsman
is_a unique_unit
cost 400 0 800 0
train_cost 20 0 50 0
population_cost 1
time_cost 100
;space 0.25
requirements imperial_age
no_auto_upgrade 1
line_upgrade 1
can_use_tech forced_levy first_crusade fereters

hp_max 75
speed 0.96
sight_range 5
mdg 12
mdg_cd 2
mdg_range 1
mdf 2
rdf 2
mdg_vs infantry 4
mdg_vs building 4
can_upgrade_to
can_use_tech forging iron_casting blast_furnace scale_mail_armor chain_mail_armor squires conscription comitatenses

def counterweights
class upgrade
cost 500 0 650 0
time_cost 50

effect bonus mdg 15%

def bimaristan
class upgrade
cost 200 300 0 0
time_cost 50
requirements aoe_castle castle_age
; 萨拉森 Bimaristan — DE: Monks passively heal multiple nearby units
effect bonus heal_level 1
effect_bonus_targets monk missionary
effect bonus heal_radius 3
effect_bonus_targets monk missionary

def citadels
class upgrade
cost 300 600 0 0
time_cost 50
requirements aoe_castle imperial_age
; 波斯 Citadels — DE: Castles +4 attack, +3 vs. Rams, +3 vs. Infantry
; and receive -25% bonus damage.
; NB: the castle attacks RANGED (rdg), so the attack bonuses must be
; ``rdg`` / ``rdg_vs`` — a ``mdg`` / ``mdg_vs`` row would never fire.
; ``effect bonus <stat> <target> <value>`` takes exactly ONE target per
; row; the ram line is covered by ``battering_ram`` because the vs-lookup
; walks ``expanded_is_a`` (``capped_ram`` / ``siege_ram`` inherit it).
effect bonus rdg 4
effect_bonus_targets aoe_castle
effect bonus rdg_vs infantry 3
effect_bonus_targets aoe_castle
effect bonus rdg_vs battering_ram 3
effect_bonus_targets aoe_castle
; DE: "receive -25% bonus damage" -> +25% armor (both melee and ranged),
; same convention the Sicilian "receive -40% bonus damage" bonus uses.
effect bonus mdf 25%
effect_bonus_targets aoe_castle
effect bonus rdf 25%
effect_bonus_targets aoe_castle

time_cost 70
